# Ouronet REPL Test Suite — Scale & Coverage

Generated by `cd REPL && python3 _suite_stats.py --gate` on 2026-09-13.

**This file computes nothing.** Every figure is lifted from the tool that owns it, named beside each section, so any number here can be re-derived independently. A second implementation would drift from the first and both would look authoritative.

---

<sub>source: `python3 _scale_report.py`</sub>

| | |
|---|---:|
| `.repl` files | **209** |
| total lines | **93,940** |
| &nbsp;&nbsp;code | 74,799 |
| &nbsp;&nbsp;comment | 17,507 |
| &nbsp;&nbsp;blank | 1,634 |

| area | lines |
|---|---:|

## 2. How many assertions

<sub>executed: most recent green gate output (2026-09-13, `wall 217.4s`) — re-run with `--gate` for a live count · distinct: counted from source</sub>

Two different questions, two different numbers — quoting the wrong one overstates the suite by ~5x.

| | |
|---|---:|
| **distinct assertions written** | **3,872** |
| assertions **executed** per full gate run | **19,373** |
| &nbsp;&nbsp;positive (`expect`) | 15,572 |
| &nbsp;&nbsp;negative (`expect-failure`) | 3,801 |
| gate entrypoints | 76 |

*Executed* exceeds *distinct* because shared files run once per entrypoint that loads them. **Quote the distinct figure for "how many tests exist"**; the executed figure answers "how much ran".

### Assertion strength

<sub>source: `python3 _expectfail.py`, `python3 _vacuous.py`</sub>

| | |
|---|---:|
| negative assertions checking their message | **976** |
| negative assertions accepting ANY error | 35 *(all in ungated scratch files)* |
| positive assertions that **cannot fail** | **0** |
| positive assertions on a trivially-satisfied bound | 10 *(reviewed, legitimate)* |

## 3. How many module functions are tested

<sub>source: `python3 _scale_report.py --functions`</sub>

Two denominators, and mixing them is how this gets misreported. **All defined functions** includes the dead DPMF module and the internal-only `XI_`/`XB_`/`XE_`/`W*_` families that no test can call by design. **Client-reachable** excludes both, and is the honest denominator for a coverage percentage.

| | all defined | client-reachable |
|---|---:|---:|
| functions | **5,422** | **4,498** |
| named directly by a test | 1,830 | 1,816 |
| reached only *through* another function | 2,122 | 1,414 |
| **reached at all** | 3,952 | **3,230 (72%)** |
| never reached | 1,470 | 1,268 |

**Total function invocations across the suite, duplicates included: 11,781.** That is every call site in every `.repl`, so a function called in 20 files counts 20 times — it answers "how much testing happens", not "how much is covered".

| 500+ | 2 |

## 4. Other coverage dimensions

**The live worklist reached 0 on 2026-09-13** — every enforce in every live module either has a
negative test pinning its exact message, or carries a `;;UNREACHABLE` annotation with an executed
assertion behind it. The three "untestable from a REPL" rows are real guards sitting behind
`require-capability` in protected bodies: not dead, just out of reach of any external caller.

The **live worklist** is the figure to size remaining work by. It is NOT `_cheapseam.py`'s count, which covers only guards in plain callable functions and is about six times smaller. The excluded rows below are already outside the denominator -- they are listed so the subtraction is visible rather than taken on trust.

| dimension | figure | tool |
|---|---:|---|
| guards pinned by a negative test (unambiguous) | 698 | `_enforce_coverage.py` |
| guards pinned (upper bound, incl. shared wording) | 752 / 777 (96%) | `_enforce_coverage.py` |
| **guards still to pin (live worklist)** | **0** — phase closed 2026-09-13 | `_enforce_coverage.py` |
| &nbsp;&nbsp;excluded: in the DEAD `00_DPMF` module | 25 | |
| &nbsp;&nbsp;excluded: unreachable inside an `enforce-one` | 40 | |
| &nbsp;&nbsp;excluded: proven unreachable and annotated | 31 | |
| &nbsp;&nbsp;excluded: live but untestable from a REPL (protected body) | 3 | |
| &nbsp;&nbsp;excluded: message is all `{}` holes (unmatchable) | 2 | |
| `@doc` AUTHORITY claims | 15 (all verified) | `_docclaims.py` |
| `@doc` IMMUTABLE claims | 5 (all verified) | `_docclaims.py` |
| `@doc` BOUND claims | 7 (all verified) | `_docclaims.py` |
| `@doc` INVARIANT claims | 73 (all verified) | `_docclaims.py` |
| **`@doc` claims with NO test** | **0** — closed 2026-09-13 | `_docclaims.py` |
| conformance violations | 0 | `_conformance.py` |
| conformance observations | 114 (all bounded + ruled) | `_conformance.py` |
| conformance observations | 117 | `_conformance.py` |
